\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Carrier, source := topology, subset := targetShell].With[coefficients := Product.integerCoefficients].At[z := I.Int^::ofNat n] \as Input;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Carrier, source := topology, subset := boundary].With[coefficients := Product.integerCoefficients].At[z := I.Int^::ofNat n] \as Boundary;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Open.Carrier, source := Open.topology, subset := Open.boundary].With[coefficients := Product.integerCoefficients].At[z := I.Int^::ofNat n] \as Output;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Open.Carrier, source := Open.topology, subset := sourceShell].With[coefficients := Product.integerCoefficients].At[z := I.Int^::ofNat n] \as ShellOutput;
\definition action[f: Maps.Map]: _ := Maps.On[f := f].Integer[].With[coefficients := Product.integerCoefficients].At[z := I.Int^::ofNat n].induced;
\definition inclusionLaw: \forall (v: Input.Carrier) -> SourceInclude.Degree[n := n].map.apply (ShellPull.Degree[n := n].map.apply v) = BoundaryPull.Degree[n := n].map.apply (TargetInclude.Degree[n := n].map.apply v) :=
  \fun (v: Input.Carrier) => trans!{Output.Carrier} _ _ _
    (sym!{Output.Carrier} _ _ (First.On[f := Direct.forward, g := ShellPairs.forwardMap pair].With[coefficients := Product.integerCoefficients].law (I.Int^::ofNat n) v))
    (trans!{Output.Carrier} _ _ _ (congr!{Maps.Map Output.Carrier} (\fun (f: Maps.Map) => (action f).apply v) _ _ square)
      (Second.On[f := BoundaryPairs.forwardMap Coordinates.pair, g := targetInclude].With[coefficients := Product.integerCoefficients].law (I.Int^::ofNat n) v));
\definition clipLaw: \forall (v: Input.Carrier) -> SourceClip.Degree[n := n].map.apply (BoundaryPull.Degree[n := n].map.apply (TargetInclude.Degree[n := n].map.apply v)) = ShellPull.Degree[n := n].map.apply v :=
  \fun (v: Input.Carrier) => trans!{ShellOutput.Carrier} _ _ _
    (congr!{Output.Carrier ShellOutput.Carrier} SourceClip.Degree[n := n].map.apply _ _ (sym!{Output.Carrier} _ _ (inclusionLaw v)))
    (Direct.With[coefficients := Product.integerCoefficients, z := I.Int^::ofNat n].isomorphism.leftInverse (ShellPull.Degree[n := n].map.apply v));
\definition normalized: \forall (u: Boundary.Carrier)(v: Input.Carrier) -> TargetInclude.Degree[n := n].map.apply v = u -> SourceClip.Degree[n := n].map.apply (BoundaryPull.Degree[n := n].map.apply u) = ShellPull.Degree[n := n].map.apply v :=
  \fun (u: Boundary.Carrier)(v: Input.Carrier)(normal: TargetInclude.Degree[n := n].map.apply v = u) => trans!{ShellOutput.Carrier} _ _ _
    (congr!{Boundary.Carrier ShellOutput.Carrier} (\fun (w: Boundary.Carrier) => SourceClip.Degree[n := n].map.apply (BoundaryPull.Degree[n := n].map.apply w)) _ _ (sym!{Boundary.Carrier} _ _ normal)) (clipLaw v);

\use std.Logic[].Equality[].eq_reason_in;
\import \root.Thom[].Real[].For[X := X, F := F, bundle := bundle].With[metric := metric].Chart[c := c].ProductCoordinates[] \as Coordinates;
\import Shell.Direct[].Domain[U := c.domain] \as Direct;
\import singular_products.Cochains[A := Carrier, source := topology, R := K, ring := ring] \as Product;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Carrier, source := topology, subset := boundary].Maps[B := Carrier].Over[target := topology, targetSubset := targetShell] \as InclusionMaps;
\definition targetInclude: InclusionMaps.Map := \into[Carrier -> Carrier]((\fun (p: Carrier) => p), InclusionMaps.Map) \by {
  InclusionMaps.Laws[(\fun (p: Carrier) => p)] {
    continuous := topology.Maps[A := Carrier, B := Carrier].Identity[].continuous topology,
    contained := \fun (p: \Cast[Carrier] boundary) => D.ltReplaceRight R.half R.one (Euclidean.Geometry[].norm (Pair.second BasePoint Euclidean.Disk p))
      (sym!{R.Real} _ _ (trans!{R.Real} _ _ _ (congr!{R.Real R.Real} R.SquareRoot[].sqrt _ _ (\bysub(Carrier, boundary, p))) R.SquareRoot[].sqrtOne)) Disk.ThomPair[].Collar[].halfBelowOne
  }
};
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Open.Carrier, source := Open.topology, subset := Open.boundary].Homeomorphism[B := Carrier].Over[target := topology, targetSubset := boundary] \as BoundaryPairs;
\import \root.RelativePullback[].On[A := Open.Carrier, source := Open.topology, subset := Open.boundary, R := K, ring := ring].To[B := Carrier, target := topology, part := boundary].Along[f := BoundaryPairs.forwardMap Coordinates.pair] \as BoundaryPull;
\import \root.RelativePullback[].On[A := Open.Carrier, source := Open.topology, subset := sourceShell, R := K, ring := ring].To[B := Carrier, target := topology, part := targetShell].Along[f := ShellPairs.forwardMap pair] \as ShellPull;
\import \root.RelativePullback[].On[A := Open.Carrier, source := Open.topology, subset := Open.boundary, R := K, ring := ring].To[B := Open.Carrier, target := Open.topology, part := sourceShell].Along[f := Direct.forward] \as SourceInclude;
\import \root.RelativePullback[].On[A := Open.Carrier, source := Open.topology, subset := sourceShell, R := K, ring := ring].To[B := Open.Carrier, target := Open.topology, part := Open.boundary].Along[f := Direct.backward] \as SourceClip;
\import \root.RelativePullback[].On[A := Carrier, source := topology, subset := boundary, R := K, ring := ring].To[B := Carrier, target := topology, part := targetShell].Along[f := targetInclude] \as TargetInclude;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Open.Carrier, source := Open.topology, subset := Open.boundary].Maps[B := Open.Carrier].Over[target := Open.topology, targetSubset := sourceShell].Composition[C := Carrier].Over[final := topology, finalSubset := targetShell] \as First;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Open.Carrier, source := Open.topology, subset := Open.boundary].Maps[B := Carrier].Over[target := topology, targetSubset := boundary].Composition[C := Carrier].Over[final := topology, finalSubset := targetShell] \as Second;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Open.Carrier, source := Open.topology, subset := Open.boundary].Maps[B := Carrier].Over[target := topology, targetSubset := targetShell] \as Maps;
\definition square: First.compose Direct.forward (ShellPairs.forwardMap pair) = Second.compose (BoundaryPairs.forwardMap Coordinates.pair) targetInclude := \refl(First.compose Direct.forward (ShellPairs.forwardMap pair));
\module Degree(n: N.Nat^);


===== /tmp/c03-local-product-comparison/Chart/ProductCoordinates/Norm.ref sha256=0abffb7416e095da06fac3cf5223fc7ef7cfac0f5eb5b15da061501b22f0b871 =====
\definition squared: \forall (p: Open.Carrier) -> Euclidean.normSquared (Pair.second BasePoint Euclidean.Disk (homeomorphism.forward p)) = Disk.squared p :=
  \fun (p: Open.Carrier) => trans!{R.Real} _ _ _ (Levels.forwardSquared (Open.backward p)) (Open.normBackward p);
\definition forward: \forall (p: Open.Carrier) -> Euclidean.Geometry[].norm (Pair.second BasePoint Euclidean.Disk (homeomorphism.forward p)) = Disk.norm p :=
  \fun (p: Open.Carrier) => congr!{R.Real R.Real} R.SquareRoot[].sqrt _ _ (squared p);
\definition backward: \forall (p: Carrier) -> Disk.norm (homeomorphism.backward p) = Euclidean.Geometry[].norm (Pair.second BasePoint Euclidean.Disk p) :=
  \fun (p: Carrier) => trans!{R.Real} _ _ _ (sym!{R.Real} _ _ (forward (homeomorphism.backward p)))
    (congr!{Carrier R.Real} (\fun (q: Carrier) => Euclidean.Geometry[].norm (Pair.second BasePoint Euclidean.Disk q)) _ _
      ((topology.Homeomorphism[A := Open.Carrier, B := Carrier].laws Open.topology topology homeomorphism).rightInverse p));
\import \root.Thom[].Real[].For[X := X, F := F, bundle := bundle].With[metric := metric].Shell[] \as Shell;
\definition sourceShell: \Pow Open.Carrier := { p: Open.Carrier \where \In[Disk.DiskBundle] Shell.shell p };
\definition targetShell: \Pow Carrier := { p: Carrier \where D.Lt R.half (Euclidean.Geometry[].norm (Pair.second BasePoint Euclidean.Disk p)) };
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Open.Carrier, source := Open.topology, subset := sourceShell].Homeomorphism[B := Carrier].Over[target := topology, targetSubset := targetShell] \as ShellPairs;
\definition pair: ShellPairs.Data := ShellPairs.Data {
  homeomorphism := homeomorphism,
  forwardPreserves := \fun (p: \Cast[Open.Carrier] sourceShell) => D.ltReplaceRight R.half (Disk.norm p) (Euclidean.Geometry[].norm (Pair.second BasePoint Euclidean.Disk (homeomorphism.forward p))) (sym!{R.Real} _ _ (forward p)) (\bysub(Open.Carrier, sourceShell, p)),
  backwardPreserves := \fun (p: \Cast[Carrier] targetShell) => D.ltReplaceRight R.half (Euclidean.Geometry[].norm (Pair.second BasePoint Euclidean.Disk p)) (Disk.norm (homeomorphism.backward p)) (sym!{R.Real} _ _ (backward p)) (\bysub(Carrier, targetShell, p))
};

\definition law: \forall (a: RightPoint.Class) -> RightPull.Degree[n := d.dimension].map.apply (class a) = AlongPull.Degree[n := d.dimension].map.apply (StandardPull.Degree[n := d.dimension].map.apply (RightStandardClass.lift a)) :=
  \fun (a: RightPoint.Class) => eq_reason_in!{Output.Carrier _
    "=" _ "by" { RightComposition.On[f := rightPartMap, g := RightDirect.backward].With[coefficients := BaseProduct.integerCoefficients].law (I.Int^::ofNat d.dimension) (class a) }
    "=" _ "by" { congr!{{singular_cohomology.Relative[A := RightOpen.Carrier, source := RightOpen.topology, subset := RightNorm.sourceShell].With[coefficients := BaseProduct.integerCoefficients].At[z := I.Int^::ofNat d.dimension].Carrier} Output.Carrier} PartPull.Degree[n := d.dimension].map.apply _ _ (RightNormRing.Degree[n := d.dimension].clipLaw (ProjectionPull.Degree[n := d.dimension].map.apply (RightStandardClass.lift a))) }

    "=" _ "by" { sym!{Output.Carrier} _ _ (ProjectedComposition.On[f := coordinates, g := projection].With[coefficients := BaseProduct.integerCoefficients].law (I.Int^::ofNat d.dimension) (RightStandardClass.lift a)) }
    "=" _ "by" { congr!{Maps.Map Output.Carrier} (\fun (f: Maps.Map) => (action f).apply (RightStandardClass.lift a)) _ _ square }
    "=" _ "by" { StandardComposition.On[f := Along.secondMap, g := StandardProduct.rightMap].With[coefficients := BaseProduct.integerCoefficients].law (I.Int^::ofNat d.dimension) (RightStandardClass.lift a) }
  };

Register `\module Direct;` under With/Shell.ref and copy Shell/Direct.ref and Direct/Domain.ref there.
Register `\module Norm;` under Chart/ProductCoordinates.ref and copy the Norm subtree there.
Add `\module Ring(K: \Set, ring: std.Alg[].Ring[].CommutativeRing[K]);` after the geometric pair declaration in Norm.ref.
Norm.Ring.Degree.clipLaw proves, for every target shell class v:
sourceClip^* hBoundary^* jTarget^* v = hShell^* v.
It uses the actual source shell inclusion/clip pair equivalence and the exact identity-inclusion square. It does not assert pointwise clip-coordinate commutation.
normalized is the useful form when v is the standard shell inverse of a specified boundary class.
The source domain shell equivalence is genuine restriction of the global radial deformation over U; it carries no Thom or primitiveness assumptions.
Drafts are unchecked; repository untouched.

Register root `\module StandardShell(n: std.Data[].Nat[].Nat^);` and copy StandardShell.ref and its directory.
This constructs a fresh geometry-only point metric (trivial bundle, identity embedding, positive metric) and transports its actual Shell.Direct inclusion iso through the actual chart norm-preserving homeomorphism to Euclidean.Disk.
No Examples.Point, global Thom, finite CW or Gysin instance is imported.
StandardShell.With[coefficients,z].isomorphism has the ACTUAL Euclidean boundary→shell identity pullback as forward map, in all integer degrees.
StandardShell.Ring[K,ring].lift transports arbitrary LocalDisk.Point rank-n classes to standard shell classes. normalized identifies their actual boundary restrictions; shellGenerator is the lift of the genuine canonical point generator.
These definitions match the intended inverse-shell standard MixedCup product and need no pointwise clipping comparison.

Register root `\module StandardProduct(n, m: std.Data[].Nat[].Nat^, K: \Set, ring: std.Alg[].Ring[].CommutativeRing[K]);` and copy StandardProduct.ref.
StandardProduct.apply takes arbitrary LocalDisk.Point classes in ranks n,m and constructs their actual mixed shell cup on Dn x Dm, then boundary inclusion and C02 round-pair pullback to D^(n+m). It assumes no primitiveness.

Register `\module Product(K: \Set, ring: Rings.CommutativeRing[K]);` under both DirectSum.With.Local and Local.Refine and copy the DirectSum/With/Local/Product subtree under both.
Product.apply is an actual local sum product for arbitrary local factor relative classes, with actual clipped pair maps.
Product.LeftFactor.class/RightFactor.class pull arbitrary standard point classes to local factor boundary classes via standard inverse shell classes and actual norm-preserving coordinate maps.
Product.Comparison.law proves that their local product equals round-coordinate pullback of StandardProduct.apply.
The only nondefinitional point comparison in the final square is the already proved C02 DiskProduct homeomorphism right inverse. Exact clip-coordinate equality is never used.
Normalization of global factor Thom classes to these local classes is intentionally separate; no normalization/primitive theorem is assumed as a module parameter.

\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Local.Carrier, source := Local.topology, subset := localShell].Maps[B := Local.Carrier].Over[target := Local.topology, targetSubset := Local.boundary] \as Backward;

\definition shellClip: ShellEnd.Map := Backward.Composition[C := Local.Carrier].Over[final := Local.topology, finalSubset := localShell].compose backward forward;
\definition equivalence: Equivalences.Equivalence := Equivalences.Equivalence {

    \fun (t: J.Carrier)(p: \Cast[Local.Carrier] Local.boundary) => trans!{R.Real} _ _ _ (congr!{Disk.DiskBundle R.Real} (\fun (q: Disk.DiskBundle) => Disk.squared q) _ _ (fixed (J.reverse t) (\into[Disk.DiskBundle](p, Disk.Boundary) \by { \bysub(Local.Carrier, Local.boundary, p) }))) (\bysub(Local.Carrier, Local.boundary, p))
  },
  right := \into[LocalHomotopies.Homotopy[Local.topology, Local.topology, localClip, (\fun (p: Local.Carrier) => p)]](localReverse, ShellEnd.Homotopy[f := shellClip, g := ShellIdentity.map].Homotopy) \by {
    \fun (t: J.Carrier)(p: \Cast[Local.Carrier] localShell) => preservesShell (J.reverse t) (\into[Disk.DiskBundle](p, shell) \by { \bysub(Local.Carrier, localShell, p) })
  }
};

\definition boundaryComparison: _ := BoundaryPairs.With[coefficients := coefficients].At[z := z].isomorphism boundaryPair;

\definition action[f: DirectMaps.Map]: _ := DirectMaps.On[f := f].Integer[].With[coefficients := coefficients].At[z := z].induced;

    "=" _ "by" { congr!{SourceBoundary.Carrier TargetBoundary.Carrier} boundaryComparison.backward.apply _ _ (sym!{SourceBoundary.Carrier} _ _ (squareLaw (shellComparison.backward.apply v))) }
    "=" _ "by" { congr!{SourceShell.Carrier TargetBoundary.Carrier} (\fun (w: SourceShell.Carrier) => boundaryComparison.backward.apply (source.forward.apply w)) _ _ (shellComparison.rightInverse v) }
  };

  topology.Composition[A := Disk.DiskBundle, B := Coordinates.Carrier, C := Euclidean.Disk].composeContinuous Disk.diskTopology Coordinates.topology Euclidean.diskTopology model (Pair.second BasePoint Euclidean.Disk)
    (topology.Composition[A := Disk.DiskBundle, B := Open.Carrier, C := Coordinates.Carrier].composeContinuous Disk.diskTopology Open.topology Coordinates.topology wrap Coordinates.homeomorphism.forward wrapContinuous
      (topology.Homeomorphism[A := Open.Carrier, B := Coordinates.Carrier].laws Open.topology Coordinates.topology Coordinates.homeomorphism).forwardContinuous)
    (topological_constructions.Product[A := BasePoint, B := Euclidean.Disk].Maps[].secondContinuous Chart.baseTopology Euclidean.diskTopology);
\definition fillContinuous: topology.Continuity[A := Euclidean.Disk, B := Coordinates.Carrier].Continuous Euclidean.diskTopology Coordinates.topology fill :=
  topological_constructions.Product[A := BasePoint, B := Euclidean.Disk].Universal[X := Euclidean.Disk].pairContinuous Euclidean.diskTopology Chart.baseTopology Euclidean.diskTopology (\fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => center) (\fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => v)
    (topology.Maps[A := Euclidean.Disk, B := BasePoint].constantContinuous Euclidean.diskTopology Chart.baseTopology center) (topology.Maps[A := Euclidean.Disk, B := Euclidean.Disk].Identity[].continuous Euclidean.diskTopology);
\definition backwardContinuous: topology.Continuity[A := Euclidean.Disk, B := Disk.DiskBundle].Continuous Euclidean.diskTopology Disk.diskTopology backward :=
  topology.Composition[A := Euclidean.Disk, B := Open.Carrier, C := Disk.DiskBundle].composeContinuous Euclidean.diskTopology Open.topology Disk.diskTopology (\fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => Coordinates.homeomorphism.backward (fill v)) Open.inclusion
    (topology.Composition[A := Euclidean.Disk, B := Coordinates.Carrier, C := Open.Carrier].composeContinuous Euclidean.diskTopology Coordinates.topology Open.topology fill Coordinates.homeomorphism.backward fillContinuous
      (topology.Homeomorphism[A := Open.Carrier, B := Coordinates.Carrier].laws Open.topology Coordinates.topology Coordinates.homeomorphism).backwardContinuous) Open.inclusionContinuous;
\definition filled: \forall (p: Disk.DiskBundle) -> fill (forward p) = model p :=
  \fun (p: Disk.DiskBundle) => Pair.Prop[A := BasePoint, B := Euclidean.Disk].ext (fill (forward p)) (model p) (sym!{BasePoint} _ _ (Point.unique (Pair.first BasePoint Euclidean.Disk (model p)))) (\refl(forward p));
\definition leftInverse: \forall (p: Disk.DiskBundle) -> backward (forward p) = p :=
  \fun (p: Disk.DiskBundle) => trans!{{Disk.DiskBundle}} _ _ _
    (congr!{Coordinates.Carrier {Disk.DiskBundle}} (\fun (v: Coordinates.Carrier) => Coordinates.homeomorphism.backward v) _ _ (filled p))
    ((topology.Homeomorphism[A := Open.Carrier, B := Coordinates.Carrier].laws Open.topology Coordinates.topology Coordinates.homeomorphism).leftInverse (wrap p));
\definition rightInverse: \forall (v: Euclidean.Disk) -> forward (backward v) = v :=
  \fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => congr!{Coordinates.Carrier {Euclidean.Disk}} (Pair.second BasePoint Euclidean.Disk) _ _
    ((topology.Homeomorphism[A := Open.Carrier, B := Coordinates.Carrier].laws Open.topology Coordinates.topology Coordinates.homeomorphism).rightInverse (fill v));
\definition homeomorphism: topology.Homeomorphism[A := Disk.DiskBundle, B := Euclidean.Disk].Homeomorphism[Disk.diskTopology, Euclidean.diskTopology] :=
  topology.Homeomorphism[A := Disk.DiskBundle, B := Euclidean.Disk].Homeomorphism[Disk.diskTopology, Euclidean.diskTopology] {
    forward := forward, backward := backward, forwardContinuous := forwardContinuous, backwardContinuous := backwardContinuous, leftInverse := leftInverse, rightInverse := rightInverse
  };
\definition forwardSquared: \forall (p: Disk.DiskBundle) -> Euclidean.normSquared (forward p) = Disk.squared p := \fun (p: Disk.DiskBundle) => Norm.squared (wrap p);
\definition backwardSquared: \forall (v: Euclidean.Disk) -> Disk.squared (backward v) = Euclidean.normSquared v :=
  \fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => trans!{R.Real} _ _ _ (sym!{R.Real} _ _ (forwardSquared (backward v))) (congr!{{Euclidean.Disk} R.Real} Euclidean.normSquared _ _ (rightInverse v));
\definition forwardNorm: \forall (p: Disk.DiskBundle) -> Euclidean.Geometry[].norm (forward p) = Disk.norm p := \fun (p: Disk.DiskBundle) => congr!{R.Real R.Real} R.SquareRoot[].sqrt _ _ (forwardSquared p);
\definition backwardNorm: \forall (v: Euclidean.Disk) -> Disk.norm (backward v) = Euclidean.Geometry[].norm v := \fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => congr!{R.Real R.Real} R.SquareRoot[].sqrt _ _ (backwardSquared v);
\definition boundary: \Pow Euclidean.Disk := { v: Euclidean.Disk \where Euclidean.normSquared v = R.one };
\definition shell: \Pow Euclidean.Disk := { v: Euclidean.Disk \where D.Lt R.half (Euclidean.Geometry[].norm v) };
\definition normContinuous: metric_spaces.RealFunctions[A := Euclidean.Disk].Continuous Euclidean.diskTopology (\fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => Euclidean.Geometry[].norm v) :=
  topology.Continuity[A := Euclidean.Coordinate, B := R.Real].Restriction[S := Euclidean.ClosedBall].restrictContinuous Euclidean.topology metric_spaces.RealLine[].topology Euclidean.Geometry[].norm Euclidean.Geometry[].normContinuous;
\definition open: topology.Topology[Carrier := Euclidean.Disk].IsOpenIn Euclidean.diskTopology shell := normContinuous (metric_spaces.RealLine[].Rays[].Above R.half) (metric_spaces.RealLine[].Rays[].aboveOpen R.half);
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Disk.DiskBundle, source := Disk.diskTopology, subset := Disk.Boundary].Homeomorphism[B := Euclidean.Disk].Over[target := Euclidean.diskTopology, targetSubset := boundary] \as BoundaryPairs;
\definition boundaryPair: BoundaryPairs.Data := BoundaryPairs.Data {
  homeomorphism := homeomorphism,
  forwardPreserves := \fun (p: \Cast[Disk.DiskBundle] Disk.Boundary) => trans!{R.Real} _ _ _ (forwardSquared p) (\bysub(Disk.DiskBundle, Disk.Boundary, p)),
  backwardPreserves := \fun (v: \Cast[Euclidean.Disk] boundary) => trans!{R.Real} _ _ _ (backwardSquared v) (\bysub(Euclidean.Disk, boundary, v))
};
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Disk.DiskBundle, source := Disk.diskTopology, subset := Shell.shell].Homeomorphism[B := Euclidean.Disk].Over[target := Euclidean.diskTopology, targetSubset := shell] \as ShellPairs;
\definition shellPair: ShellPairs.Data := ShellPairs.Data {
  homeomorphism := homeomorphism,
  forwardPreserves := \fun (p: \Cast[Disk.DiskBundle] Shell.shell) => D.ltReplaceRight R.half (Disk.norm p) (Euclidean.Geometry[].norm (forward p)) (sym!{R.Real} _ _ (forwardNorm p)) (\bysub(Disk.DiskBundle, Shell.shell, p)),
  backwardPreserves := \fun (v: \Cast[Euclidean.Disk] shell) => D.ltReplaceRight R.half (Euclidean.Geometry[].norm v) (Disk.norm (backward v)) (sym!{R.Real} _ _ (backwardNorm v)) (\bysub(Euclidean.Disk, shell, v))
};
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Euclidean.Disk, source := Euclidean.diskTopology, subset := boundary].Maps[B := Euclidean.Disk].Over[target := Euclidean.diskTopology, targetSubset := shell] \as Include;
\definition include: Include.Map := \into[Euclidean.Disk -> Euclidean.Disk]((\fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => v), Include.Map) \by {
  Include.Laws[(\fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => v)] { continuous := topology.Maps[A := Euclidean.Disk, B := Euclidean.Disk].Identity[].continuous Euclidean.diskTopology,
    contained := \fun (v: \Cast[Euclidean.Disk] boundary) => D.ltReplaceRight R.half R.one (Euclidean.Geometry[].norm v)
      (sym!{R.Real} _ _ (trans!{R.Real} _ _ _ (congr!{R.Real R.Real} R.SquareRoot[].sqrt _ _ (\bysub(Euclidean.Disk, boundary, v))) R.SquareRoot[].sqrtOne)) Disk.ThomPair[].Collar[].halfBelowOne
  }
};
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Disk.DiskBundle, source := Disk.diskTopology, subset := Disk.Boundary].Maps[B := Disk.DiskBundle].Over[target := Disk.diskTopology, targetSubset := Shell.shell].Composition[C := Euclidean.Disk].Over[final := Euclidean.diskTopology, finalSubset := shell] \as FirstComposition;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Disk.DiskBundle, source := Disk.diskTopology, subset := Disk.Boundary].Maps[B := Euclidean.Disk].Over[target := Euclidean.diskTopology, targetSubset := boundary].Composition[C := Euclidean.Disk].Over[final := Euclidean.diskTopology, finalSubset := shell] \as SecondComposition;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Disk.DiskBundle, source := Disk.diskTopology, subset := Disk.Boundary].Maps[B := Euclidean.Disk].Over[target := Euclidean.diskTopology, targetSubset := shell] \as DirectMaps;
\definition square: FirstComposition.compose Direct.forward (ShellPairs.forwardMap shellPair) = SecondComposition.compose (BoundaryPairs.forwardMap boundaryPair) include := \refl(FirstComposition.compose Direct.forward (ShellPairs.forwardMap shellPair));
\module With(coefficients: Modules.Bundle, z: I.Int^);
\module PointModel;
\module Ring(K: \Set, ring: std.Alg[].Ring[].CommutativeRing[K]);


===== /tmp/c03-standard-product-step/INTEGRATION.md sha256=d8fc2f30c6fa4fabfc26d53f6482e3f4d646862d7ee44fea3cc3e739a993d644 =====
Register root modules:
```
\module StandardDiskStep(m: std.Data[].Nat[].Nat^);
\module StandardProductStep(n, m: std.Data[].Nat[].Nat^);
```
StandardDiskStep composes the pure Euclidean-to-point disk adapter, existing checked LocalDisk.On[Point].Step round/cylinder pair homeomorphisms, and point erasure to obtain D^(m+1) -> I x D^m, preserving the actual sphere <-> endpoints union lower sphere.
StandardProductStep keeps D^n unchanged and rebrackets to I x (D^n x D^m).
It preserves the left-boundary family member and the suspended right-boundary family member separately in both directions, and builds actual ClassifiedFamilyMap forward/backward maps.
The homeomorphism gives explicit point inverse formulas (leftInverse/rightInverse) for parent FamilyPairFunctor/chain/coho isomorphism construction. No isomorphism is assumed as a theorem parameter.
These drafts are unchecked. No repository changes/checkers/Git operations were performed.

\import std.Data[].Nat[] \as N;
\import std.Logic[].Proposition[] \as Logic;
\import std.Logic[].Equality[] \as Equality;
\use Equality.trans;
\use Equality.sym;
\use Equality.congr;
\use Logic.either;
\import std.Data[].Pair[] \as Pair;
\import real.Analysis[] \as R;
\import thom_spaces.Thom[].CWType[].Point[] \as Point;
\import algebraic_topology.Interval[] \as J;
\import algebraic_topology.Euclidean[].Dimension[n := m] \as Lower;
\import algebraic_topology.Euclidean[].Dimension[n := N.Nat^::succ m] \as Higher;
\import \root.StandardShell[n := m].PointModel[] \as LowerPoint;
\import \root.StandardShell[n := N.Nat^::succ m].PointModel[] \as HigherPoint;
\import thom_spaces.Thom[].DiskSuspension[X := Point.Carrier, space := Point.topology, n := m] \as LowerDisk;
\import thom_spaces.Thom[].DiskSuspension[X := Point.Carrier, space := Point.topology, n := N.Nat^::succ m] \as HigherDisk;
\import LowerDisk.Step[] \as Shape;
\import \root.LocalDisk[].On[X := Point.Carrier, source := Point.topology].Step[n := m] \as Native;
\import algebraic_topology.Cylinder[A := LowerDisk.DiskCarrier] \as PointCylinder;
\import algebraic_topology.Cylinder[A := Lower.Disk] \as Cylinder;
\definition topology: topology.Topology[Carrier := Cylinder.Carrier].Topology := Cylinder.topology Lower.diskTopology;
\definition boundary: \Pow Cylinder.Carrier := { p: Cylinder.Carrier \where Logic.Or[Cylinder.time p = J.start, Logic.Or[Cylinder.time p = J.end, Lower.normSquared (Cylinder.point p) = R.one]] };
\definition erase[p: PointCylinder.Carrier]: Cylinder.Carrier := Cylinder.at (PointCylinder.time p) (LowerPoint.backward (PointCylinder.point p));
\definition fill[p: Cylinder.Carrier]: PointCylinder.Carrier := PointCylinder.at (Cylinder.time p) (LowerPoint.forward (Cylinder.point p));
\definition eraseContinuous: topology.Continuity[A := PointCylinder.Carrier, B := Cylinder.Carrier].Continuous (PointCylinder.topology LowerDisk.diskTopology) topology erase :=
  topological_constructions.Product[A := J.Carrier, B := Lower.Disk].Universal[X := PointCylinder.Carrier].pairContinuous (PointCylinder.topology LowerDisk.diskTopology) J.topology Lower.diskTopology PointCylinder.time (\fun (p: PointCylinder.Carrier) => LowerPoint.backward (PointCylinder.point p))
    (PointCylinder.timeContinuous LowerDisk.diskTopology)
    (topology.Composition[A := PointCylinder.Carrier, B := LowerDisk.DiskCarrier, C := Lower.Disk].composeContinuous (PointCylinder.topology LowerDisk.diskTopology) LowerDisk.diskTopology Lower.diskTopology PointCylinder.point LowerPoint.backward (PointCylinder.pointContinuous LowerDisk.diskTopology) LowerPoint.backwardContinuous);
\definition fillContinuous: topology.Continuity[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := PointCylinder.Carrier].Continuous topology (PointCylinder.topology LowerDisk.diskTopology) fill :=
  topological_constructions.Product[A := J.Carrier, B := LowerDisk.DiskCarrier].Universal[X := Cylinder.Carrier].pairContinuous topology J.topology LowerDisk.diskTopology Cylinder.time (\fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier) => LowerPoint.forward (Cylinder.point p))
    (Cylinder.timeContinuous Lower.diskTopology)
    (topology.Composition[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := Lower.Disk, C := LowerDisk.DiskCarrier].composeContinuous topology Lower.diskTopology LowerDisk.diskTopology Cylinder.point LowerPoint.forward (Cylinder.pointContinuous Lower.diskTopology) LowerPoint.forwardContinuous);
\definition eraseHomeomorphism: topology.Homeomorphism[A := PointCylinder.Carrier, B := Cylinder.Carrier].Homeomorphism[PointCylinder.topology LowerDisk.diskTopology, topology] :=
  topology.Homeomorphism[A := PointCylinder.Carrier, B := Cylinder.Carrier].Homeomorphism[PointCylinder.topology LowerDisk.diskTopology, topology] {
    forward := erase, backward := fill, forwardContinuous := eraseContinuous, backwardContinuous := fillContinuous,
    leftInverse := \fun (p: PointCylinder.Carrier) => Pair.Prop[A := J.Carrier, B := LowerDisk.DiskCarrier].ext (fill (erase p)) p (\refl(PointCylinder.time p))
      ((topology.Homeomorphism[A := Lower.Disk, B := LowerDisk.DiskCarrier].laws Lower.diskTopology LowerDisk.diskTopology LowerPoint.homeomorphism).rightInverse (PointCylinder.point p)),
    rightInverse := \fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier) => Pair.Prop[A := J.Carrier, B := Lower.Disk].ext (erase (fill p)) p (\refl(Cylinder.time p))
      ((topology.Homeomorphism[A := Lower.Disk, B := LowerDisk.DiskCarrier].laws Lower.diskTopology LowerDisk.diskTopology LowerPoint.homeomorphism).leftInverse (Cylinder.point p))
  };
\definition pointHomeomorphism: topology.Homeomorphism[A := HigherDisk.DiskCarrier, B := PointCylinder.Carrier].Homeomorphism[HigherDisk.diskTopology, PointCylinder.topology LowerDisk.diskTopology] :=
  topology.Homeomorphism[A := HigherDisk.DiskCarrier, B := Shape.BoxCarrier].Composition[C := PointCylinder.Carrier].compose HigherDisk.diskTopology Shape.boxTopology (PointCylinder.topology LowerDisk.diskTopology) Native.round.homeomorphism Native.cylinder.homeomorphism;
\definition homeomorphism: topology.Homeomorphism[A := Higher.Disk, B := Cylinder.Carrier].Homeomorphism[Higher.diskTopology, topology] :=
  topology.Homeomorphism[A := Higher.Disk, B := PointCylinder.Carrier].Composition[C := Cylinder.Carrier].compose Higher.diskTopology (PointCylinder.topology LowerDisk.diskTopology) topology
    (topology.Homeomorphism[A := Higher.Disk, B := HigherDisk.DiskCarrier].Composition[C := PointCylinder.Carrier].compose Higher.diskTopology HigherDisk.diskTopology (PointCylinder.topology LowerDisk.diskTopology) HigherPoint.homeomorphism pointHomeomorphism) eraseHomeomorphism;
\definition forward: Higher.Disk -> Cylinder.Carrier := homeomorphism.forward;
\definition backward: Cylinder.Carrier -> Higher.Disk := homeomorphism.backward;
\definition laws: topology.Homeomorphism[A := Higher.Disk, B := Cylinder.Carrier].Laws[Higher.diskTopology, topology, homeomorphism] := topology.Homeomorphism[A := Higher.Disk, B := Cylinder.Carrier].laws Higher.diskTopology topology homeomorphism;
\import \root.RelativeSuspension[].On[A := LowerDisk.DiskCarrier, source := LowerDisk.diskTopology, subset := LowerDisk.boundary] \as Suspension;
\definition erasedBoundary: \forall (p: PointCylinder.Carrier) -> \In[PointCylinder.Carrier] Suspension.boundary p -> \In[Cylinder.Carrier] boundary (erase p) :=
  \fun (p: PointCylinder.Carrier)(inside: _) => either!{inside {\In[Cylinder.Carrier] boundary (erase p)}
    {\fun (bottom: PointCylinder.time p = J.start) => Logic.Or[_, _]::left bottom}
    {\fun (other: _) => either!{other {\In[Cylinder.Carrier] boundary (erase p)}
      {\fun (top: PointCylinder.time p = J.end) => Logic.Or[_, _]::right (Logic.Or[_, _]::left top)}
      {\fun (side: \In[LowerDisk.DiskCarrier] LowerDisk.boundary (PointCylinder.point p)) => Logic.Or[_, _]::right (Logic.Or[_, _]::right side)}}}};
\definition filledBoundary: \forall (p: Cylinder.Carrier) -> \In[Cylinder.Carrier] boundary p -> \In[PointCylinder.Carrier] Suspension.boundary (fill p) :=
  \fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier)(inside: _) => either!{inside {\In[PointCylinder.Carrier] Suspension.boundary (fill p)}
    {\fun (bottom: Cylinder.time p = J.start) => Logic.Or[_, _]::left bottom}
    {\fun (other: _) => either!{other {\In[PointCylinder.Carrier] Suspension.boundary (fill p)}
      {\fun (top: Cylinder.time p = J.end) => Logic.Or[_, _]::right (Logic.Or[_, _]::left top)}
      {\fun (side: Lower.normSquared (Cylinder.point p) = R.one) => Logic.Or[_, _]::right (Logic.Or[_, _]::right side)}}}};
\definition sourceBoundary: \Pow Higher.Disk := { v: Higher.Disk \where Higher.normSquared v = R.one };
\definition forwardBoundary: \forall (v: Higher.Disk) -> \In[Higher.Disk] sourceBoundary v -> \In[Cylinder.Carrier] boundary (forward v) :=
  \fun (v: Higher.Disk)(inside: _) => erasedBoundary (pointHomeomorphism.forward (HigherPoint.forward v))
    (Native.cylinder.forwardPreserves (\into[Shape.BoxCarrier](Native.round.homeomorphism.forward (HigherPoint.forward v), Shape.BoxBoundary) \by {
      Native.round.forwardPreserves (\into[HigherDisk.DiskCarrier](HigherPoint.forward v, HigherDisk.boundary) \by { inside })
    }));
\definition backwardBoundary: \forall (p: Cylinder.Carrier) -> \In[Cylinder.Carrier] boundary p -> \In[Higher.Disk] sourceBoundary (backward p) :=
  \fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier)(inside: _) => Native.round.backwardPreserves
    (\into[Shape.BoxCarrier](Native.cylinder.homeomorphism.backward (fill p), Shape.BoxBoundary) \by {
      Native.cylinder.backwardPreserves (\into[PointCylinder.Carrier](fill p, Suspension.boundary) \by { filledBoundary p inside })
    });


===== /tmp/c03-standard-product-step/StandardProductStep.ref sha256=f2a354002ebc810dbab66f22c67525447a016bc3730c954abd00a5f574a20f34 =====
\import std.Data[].Nat[] \as N;
\import std.Logic[].Proposition[] \as Logic;
\import std.Logic[].Equality[] \as Equality;
\use Equality.trans;
\use Equality.congr;
\use Equality.sym;
\import std.Data[].Pair[] \as Pair;
\import real.Analysis[] \as R;
\import algebraic_topology.Interval[] \as J;
\import algebraic_topology.Euclidean[].Dimension[n := n] \as Left;
\import algebraic_topology.Euclidean[].Dimension[n := m] \as Right;
\import algebraic_topology.Euclidean[].Dimension[n := N.Nat^::succ m] \as Higher;
\import \root.StandardDiskStep[m := m] \as Step;
\import algebraic_topology.Cylinder[A := Right.Disk] \as RightCylinder;
\import topological_constructions.Product[A := Left.Disk, B := Right.Disk] \as Base;
\import topological_constructions.Product[A := Left.Disk, B := Higher.Disk] \as Source;
\definition Carrier: \Set := Source.CarrierSet;
\definition topology: topology.Topology[Carrier := Carrier].Topology := Source.topology Left.diskTopology Higher.diskTopology;
\definition baseTopology: topology.Topology[Carrier := Base.CarrierSet].Topology := Base.topology Left.diskTopology Right.diskTopology;
\definition first: \Pow Carrier := { p: Carrier \where Left.normSquared (Pair.first Left.Disk Higher.Disk p) = R.one };
\definition second: \Pow Carrier := { p: Carrier \where Higher.normSquared (Pair.second Left.Disk Higher.Disk p) = R.one };
\definition family: \Pow (\Pow Carrier) := { U: \Pow Carrier \where Logic.Or[U = first, U = second] };
\definition baseFirst: \Pow Base.CarrierSet := { p: Base.CarrierSet \where Left.normSquared (Pair.first Left.Disk Right.Disk p) = R.one };
\definition baseSecond: \Pow Base.CarrierSet := { p: Base.CarrierSet \where Right.normSquared (Pair.second Left.Disk Right.Disk p) = R.one };
\import \root.FamilySuspension[A := Base.CarrierSet, source := baseTopology, first := baseFirst, second := baseSecond] \as Suspension;
\import algebraic_topology.Cylinder[A := Base.CarrierSet] \as Cylinder;
\definition targetTopology: topology.Topology[Carrier := Cylinder.Carrier].Topology := Cylinder.topology baseTopology;
\definition stage[p: Carrier]: RightCylinder.Carrier := Step.forward (Pair.second Left.Disk Higher.Disk p);
\definition forward[p: Carrier]: Cylinder.Carrier := Cylinder.at (RightCylinder.time (stage p))
  (Pair.pair Left.Disk Right.Disk (Pair.first Left.Disk Higher.Disk p) (RightCylinder.point (stage p)));
\definition rightCylinder[p: Cylinder.Carrier]: RightCylinder.Carrier := RightCylinder.at (Cylinder.time p) (Pair.second Left.Disk Right.Disk (Cylinder.point p));
\definition backward[p: Cylinder.Carrier]: Carrier := Pair.pair Left.Disk Higher.Disk (Pair.first Left.Disk Right.Disk (Cylinder.point p)) (Step.backward (rightCylinder p));
\definition stageContinuous: topology.Continuity[A := Carrier, B := RightCylinder.Carrier].Continuous topology Step.topology stage :=
  topology.Composition[A := Carrier, B := Higher.Disk, C := RightCylinder.Carrier].composeContinuous topology Higher.diskTopology Step.topology (Pair.second Left.Disk Higher.Disk) Step.forward
    (Source.Maps[].secondContinuous Left.diskTopology Higher.diskTopology) Step.laws.forwardContinuous;
\definition forwardContinuous: topology.Continuity[A := Carrier, B := Cylinder.Carrier].Continuous topology targetTopology forward :=
  topological_constructions.Product[A := J.Carrier, B := Base.CarrierSet].Universal[X := Carrier].pairContinuous topology J.topology baseTopology (\fun (p: Carrier) => RightCylinder.time (stage p))
    (\fun (p: Carrier) => Pair.pair Left.Disk Right.Disk (Pair.first Left.Disk Higher.Disk p) (RightCylinder.point (stage p)))
    (topology.Composition[A := Carrier, B := RightCylinder.Carrier, C := J.Carrier].composeContinuous topology Step.topology J.topology stage RightCylinder.time stageContinuous (RightCylinder.timeContinuous Right.diskTopology))
    (Base.Universal[X := Carrier].pairContinuous topology Left.diskTopology Right.diskTopology (Pair.first Left.Disk Higher.Disk) (\fun (p: Carrier) => RightCylinder.point (stage p))
      (Source.Maps[].firstContinuous Left.diskTopology Higher.diskTopology)
      (topology.Composition[A := Carrier, B := RightCylinder.Carrier, C := Right.Disk].composeContinuous topology Step.topology Right.diskTopology stage RightCylinder.point stageContinuous (RightCylinder.pointContinuous Right.diskTopology)));
\definition rightCylinderContinuous: topology.Continuity[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := RightCylinder.Carrier].Continuous targetTopology Step.topology rightCylinder :=
  topological_constructions.Product[A := J.Carrier, B := Right.Disk].Universal[X := Cylinder.Carrier].pairContinuous targetTopology J.topology Right.diskTopology Cylinder.time (\fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier) => Pair.second Left.Disk Right.Disk (Cylinder.point p))
    (Cylinder.timeContinuous baseTopology)
    (topology.Composition[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := Base.CarrierSet, C := Right.Disk].composeContinuous targetTopology baseTopology Right.diskTopology Cylinder.point (Pair.second Left.Disk Right.Disk) (Cylinder.pointContinuous baseTopology) (Base.Maps[].secondContinuous Left.diskTopology Right.diskTopology));
\definition backwardContinuous: topology.Continuity[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := Carrier].Continuous targetTopology topology backward :=
  Source.Universal[X := Cylinder.Carrier].pairContinuous targetTopology Left.diskTopology Higher.diskTopology (\fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier) => Pair.first Left.Disk Right.Disk (Cylinder.point p)) (\fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier) => Step.backward (rightCylinder p))
    (topology.Composition[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := Base.CarrierSet, C := Left.Disk].composeContinuous targetTopology baseTopology Left.diskTopology Cylinder.point (Pair.first Left.Disk Right.Disk) (Cylinder.pointContinuous baseTopology) (Base.Maps[].firstContinuous Left.diskTopology Right.diskTopology))
    (topology.Composition[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := RightCylinder.Carrier, C := Higher.Disk].composeContinuous targetTopology Step.topology Higher.diskTopology rightCylinder Step.backward rightCylinderContinuous Step.laws.backwardContinuous);
\definition rebuilt: \forall (p: Carrier) -> rightCylinder (forward p) = stage p := \fun (p: Carrier) => Pair.Prop[A := J.Carrier, B := Right.Disk].eta (stage p);
\definition leftInverse: \forall (p: Carrier) -> backward (forward p) = p :=
  \fun (p: Carrier) => Pair.Prop[A := Left.Disk, B := Higher.Disk].ext (backward (forward p)) p (\refl(Pair.first Left.Disk Higher.Disk p))
    (trans!{{Higher.Disk}} _ _ _ (congr!{RightCylinder.Carrier {Higher.Disk}} Step.backward _ _ (rebuilt p)) (Step.laws.leftInverse (Pair.second Left.Disk Higher.Disk p)));
\definition backStage: \forall (p: Cylinder.Carrier) -> stage (backward p) = rightCylinder p := \fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier) => Step.laws.rightInverse (rightCylinder p);
\definition rightInverse: \forall (p: Cylinder.Carrier) -> forward (backward p) = p :=
  \fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier) => Pair.Prop[A := J.Carrier, B := Base.CarrierSet].ext (forward (backward p)) p
    (congr!{RightCylinder.Carrier {J.Carrier}} RightCylinder.time _ _ (backStage p))
    (Pair.Prop[A := Left.Disk, B := Right.Disk].ext _ _ (\refl(Pair.first Left.Disk Right.Disk (Cylinder.point p))) (congr!{RightCylinder.Carrier {Right.Disk}} RightCylinder.point _ _ (backStage p)));
\definition homeomorphism: topology.Homeomorphism[A := Carrier, B := Cylinder.Carrier].Homeomorphism[topology, targetTopology] :=
  topology.Homeomorphism[A := Carrier, B := Cylinder.Carrier].Homeomorphism[topology, targetTopology] {
    forward := forward, backward := backward, forwardContinuous := forwardContinuous, backwardContinuous := backwardContinuous, leftInverse := leftInverse, rightInverse := rightInverse
  };
\definition firstForward: \forall (p: Carrier) -> \In[Carrier] first p -> \In[Cylinder.Carrier] Suspension.firstPart (forward p) := \fun (p: Carrier)(inside: _) => inside;
\definition secondForward: \forall (p: Carrier) -> \In[Carrier] second p -> \In[Cylinder.Carrier] Suspension.secondPart (forward p) :=
  \fun (p: Carrier)(inside: _) => Step.forwardBoundary (Pair.second Left.Disk Higher.Disk p) inside;
\definition firstBackward: \forall (p: Cylinder.Carrier) -> \In[Cylinder.Carrier] Suspension.firstPart p -> \In[Carrier] first (backward p) := \fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier)(inside: _) => inside;
\definition secondBackward: \forall (p: Cylinder.Carrier) -> \In[Cylinder.Carrier] Suspension.secondPart p -> \In[Carrier] second (backward p) :=
  \fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier)(inside: _) => Step.backwardBoundary (rightCylinder p) inside;
\import \root.ClassifiedFamilyMap[A := Carrier, B := Cylinder.Carrier, source := topology, target := targetTopology, sourceFirst := first, sourceSecond := second, targetFirst := Suspension.firstPart, targetSecond := Suspension.secondPart, sourceFamily := family, targetFamily := Suspension.family,
  classify := (\fun (U: \Pow Carrier)(member: \In[\Pow Carrier] family U) => member),
  targetFirstMember := Logic.Or[Suspension.firstPart = Suspension.firstPart, Suspension.firstPart = Suspension.secondPart]::left (\refl(Suspension.firstPart)),
  targetSecondMember := Logic.Or[Suspension.secondPart = Suspension.firstPart, Suspension.secondPart = Suspension.secondPart]::right (\refl(Suspension.secondPart)),
  f := forward, regular := forwardContinuous, firstContained := firstForward, secondContained := secondForward] \as Forward;
\import \root.ClassifiedFamilyMap[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := Carrier, source := targetTopology, target := topology, sourceFirst := Suspension.firstPart, sourceSecond := Suspension.secondPart, targetFirst := first, targetSecond := second, sourceFamily := Suspension.family, targetFamily := family,
  classify := (\fun (U: \Pow Cylinder.Carrier)(member: \In[\Pow Cylinder.Carrier] Suspension.family U) => member),
  targetFirstMember := Logic.Or[first = first, first = second]::left (\refl(first)),
  targetSecondMember := Logic.Or[second = first, second = second]::right (\refl(second)),
  f := backward, regular := backwardContinuous, firstContained := firstBackward, secondContained := secondBackward] \as Backward;
\definition forwardMap: _ := Forward.map;
\definition backwardMap: _ := Backward.map;
\definition forwardPreservesSmall: _ := Forward.preserves;
\definition backwardPreservesSmall: _ := Backward.preserves;

Register root `\module StandardProductShell(n, m: std.Data[].Nat[].Nat^);`.
Copy StandardProductShell root + Families/With + Pair children.

StandardShell.Motion conjugates the actual checked metric-bundle shell radial motion through StandardShell.homeomorphism. It gives actual continuous Euclidean disk motion, start, clip, fixed on boundary, preserves open shell, sends shell to boundary at endpoint.
StandardProductShell takes componentwise factor motions. Each closed face and each open-shell preimage is preserved separately throughout. Actual ClassifiedFamilyMap gives closed-family→shell-family identity and shell-family→closed-family clip.
Families.With[M].At[z].isomorphism is actual family quotient cohomology iso H(shell family)→H(closed family), forward native identity-induced pull and backward native componentwise clip-induced pull. Proof uses Composition, Equal.With.law, FamilyHomotopy.With, and FamilyPair.Identity.With.
Pair.equivalence is ordinary closed-union↔shell-union relative equivalence from the SAME identity/clip and reverse motion. Pair.With[M,z].isomorphism gives corresponding ordinary relative coho iso.

Dependency addition: /tmp/c03-family-homeo/Equal.ref now additionally declares With(coefficients:Modules.Bundle), implemented by Equal/With.ref (12 lines). This proves native induced pull equality by congruence on the actual chain-map equality; no extra assumption.

No product primitive theorem or class comparison is assumed. These bridge both versions of the standard actual product to the common-family setting; FamilyCommonModel supplies the remaining product comparison.

\import resolutions.HomComplex[].Over[R := I.Int^, ring := Integers.commutativeRing] \as Hom;
\import \root.FamilyPair[A := Carrier, source := topology, family := closedFamily] \as Closed;
\import \root.FamilyPair[A := Carrier, source := topology, family := shellFamily] \as Open;

\import Hom.Integer[P := Open.complex, N := coefficients] \as OpenCochains;

\import Backward.With[coefficients := coefficients] \as BackwardPull;
\module At(z: I.Int^) {
  \import ClosedCochains.Cohomology[z := z] \as ClosedDegree;
  \import OpenCochains.Cohomology[z := z] \as OpenDegree;
  \definition leftInverse: \forall (q: OpenDegree.Cohomology) -> (BackwardPull.At[z := z].induced).apply ((ForwardPull.At[z := z].induced).apply q) = q :=
    \fun (q: OpenDegree.Cohomology) => trans!{OpenDegree.Cohomology} _ _ _
      (sym!{OpenDegree.Cohomology} _ _ (ShellComposite.With[coefficients := coefficients].law z q))
      (trans!{OpenDegree.Cohomology} _ _ _ (ShellEquality.With[coefficients := coefficients].law z q)
        (trans!{OpenDegree.Cohomology} _ _ _ (sym!{OpenDegree.Cohomology} _ _ (ShellHomotopy.With[coefficients := coefficients].At[z := z].law q))
          (Open.Identity[].With[coefficients := coefficients].law z q)));
  \definition rightInverse: \forall (q: ClosedDegree.Cohomology) -> (ForwardPull.At[z := z].induced).apply ((BackwardPull.At[z := z].induced).apply q) = q :=
    \fun (q: ClosedDegree.Cohomology) => trans!{ClosedDegree.Cohomology} _ _ _
      (sym!{ClosedDegree.Cohomology} _ _ (ClosedComposite.With[coefficients := coefficients].law z q))
      (trans!{ClosedDegree.Cohomology} _ _ _ (ClosedEquality.With[coefficients := coefficients].law z q)
        (trans!{ClosedDegree.Cohomology} _ _ _ (sym!{ClosedDegree.Cohomology} _ _ (ClosedHomotopy.With[coefficients := coefficients].At[z := z].law q))
          (Closed.Identity[].With[coefficients := coefficients].law z q)));
  \import Modules.Isomorphism[V := OpenDegree.Cohomology, W := ClosedDegree.Cohomology, source := OpenDegree.cohomologySpace, target := ClosedDegree.cohomologySpace] \as Isomorphisms;
  \definition data: Isomorphisms.Data := Isomorphisms.Data { forward := ForwardPull.At[z := z].induced, backward := BackwardPull.At[z := z].induced };
  \definition isomorphism: Isomorphisms.Isomorphism := \into[Isomorphisms.Data](data, Isomorphisms.Isomorphism) \by { Isomorphisms.Laws[data] { leftInverse := leftInverse, rightInverse := rightInverse } };
}

\definition closedUnion: \Pow Carrier := { p: Carrier \where Logic.Or[\In[Carrier] closedFirst p, \In[Carrier] closedSecond p] };
\definition shellUnion: \Pow Carrier := { p: Carrier \where Logic.Or[\In[Carrier] shellFirst p, \In[Carrier] shellSecond p] };
\import \root.StandardProductShell[n := n, m := m].Families[] \as Families;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Carrier, source := topology, subset := closedUnion].Maps[B := Carrier].Over[target := topology, targetSubset := shellUnion] \as Forward;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Carrier, source := topology, subset := shellUnion].Maps[B := Carrier].Over[target := topology, targetSubset := closedUnion] \as Backward;
\definition intoShell: \forall (p: Carrier) -> \In[Carrier] closedUnion p -> \In[Carrier] shellUnion p :=
  \fun (p: Carrier)(inside: _) => either!{inside {\In[Carrier] shellUnion p}
    {\fun (left: \In[Carrier] closedFirst p) => Logic.Or[_, _]::left (Families.intoFirst p left)}
    {\fun (right: \In[Carrier] closedSecond p) => Logic.Or[_, _]::right (Families.intoSecond p right)}};
\definition intoBoundary: \forall (p: Carrier) -> \In[Carrier] shellUnion p -> \In[Carrier] closedUnion (clip p) :=
  \fun (p: Carrier)(inside: _) => either!{inside {\In[Carrier] closedUnion (clip p)}
    {\fun (left: \In[Carrier] shellFirst p) => Logic.Or[_, _]::left (LM.endsInBoundary (\into[Left.Disk](first p, L.shell) \by { left }))}
    {\fun (right: \In[Carrier] shellSecond p) => Logic.Or[_, _]::right (RM.endsInBoundary (\into[Right.Disk](second p, R.shell) \by { right }))}};
\definition forward: Forward.Map := \into[Carrier -> Carrier](identity, Forward.Map) \by { Forward.Laws[identity] { continuous := identityContinuous, contained := \fun (p: \Cast[Carrier] closedUnion) => intoShell p (\bysub(Carrier, closedUnion, p)) } };
\definition backward: Backward.Map := \into[Carrier -> Carrier](clip, Backward.Map) \by { Backward.Laws[clip] { continuous := clipContinuous, contained := \fun (p: \Cast[Carrier] shellUnion) => intoBoundary p (\bysub(Carrier, shellUnion, p)) } };
\definition closedPreserved: \forall (t: J.Carrier)(p: \Cast[Carrier] closedUnion) -> \In[Carrier] closedUnion (motion (Cylinder.at t p)) :=
  \fun (t: J.Carrier)(p: \Cast[Carrier] closedUnion) => either!{{\bysub(Carrier, closedUnion, p)} {\In[Carrier] closedUnion (motion (Cylinder.at t p))}
    {\fun (left: \In[Carrier] closedFirst p) => Logic.Or[_, _]::left (closedFirstPreserved t p left)}
    {\fun (right: \In[Carrier] closedSecond p) => Logic.Or[_, _]::right (closedSecondPreserved t p right)}};
\definition shellPreserved: \forall (t: J.Carrier)(p: \Cast[Carrier] shellUnion) -> \In[Carrier] shellUnion (motion (Cylinder.at t p)) :=
  \fun (t: J.Carrier)(p: \Cast[Carrier] shellUnion) => either!{{\bysub(Carrier, shellUnion, p)} {\In[Carrier] shellUnion (motion (Cylinder.at t p))}
    {\fun (left: \In[Carrier] shellFirst p) => Logic.Or[_, _]::left (shellFirstPreserved t p left)}
    {\fun (right: \In[Carrier] shellSecond p) => Logic.Or[_, _]::right (shellSecondPreserved t p right)}};
\definition reverse: Homotopies.Homotopy[topology, topology, clip, identity] := Homotopies.reverse topology topology identity clip deformation;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Carrier, source := topology, subset := closedUnion].Equivalence[B := Carrier].Over[target := topology, targetSubset := shellUnion] \as Equivalences;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Carrier, source := topology, subset := closedUnion].Maps[B := Carrier].Over[target := topology, targetSubset := closedUnion] \as ClosedEnd;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Carrier, source := topology, subset := shellUnion].Maps[B := Carrier].Over[target := topology, targetSubset := shellUnion] \as OpenEnd;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Carrier, source := topology, subset := closedUnion].Identity[] \as ClosedIdentity;
\import singular_cohomology.Relative[A := Carrier, source := topology, subset := shellUnion].Identity[] \as OpenIdentity;
\definition closedClip: ClosedEnd.Map := Forward.Composition[C := Carrier].Over[final := topology, finalSubset := closedUnion].compose forward backward;
\definition openClip: OpenEnd.Map := Backward.Composition[C := Carrier].Over[final := topology, finalSubset := shellUnion].compose backward forward;
\definition equivalence: Equivalences.Equivalence := Equivalences.Equivalence {
  forward := forward, backward := backward,
  left := \into[Homotopies.Homotopy[topology, topology, clip, identity]](reverse, ClosedEnd.Homotopy[f := closedClip, g := ClosedIdentity.map].Homotopy) \by { \fun (t: J.Carrier)(p: \Cast[Carrier] closedUnion) => closedPreserved (J.reverse t) p },
  right := \into[Homotopies.Homotopy[topology, topology, clip, identity]](reverse, OpenEnd.Homotopy[f := openClip, g := OpenIdentity.map].Homotopy) \by { \fun (t: J.Carrier)(p: \Cast[Carrier] shellUnion) => shellPreserved (J.reverse t) p }
};
\module With(coefficients: Modules.Bundle, z: I.Int^) { \definition isomorphism: _ := Equivalences.On[e := equivalence].With[coefficients := coefficients].At[z := z].isomorphism; }

\definition rightCylinder[p: Cylinder.Carrier]: RC.Carrier := RC.at (Cylinder.time p) (second (Cylinder.point p));

    (topology.Composition[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := Carrier, C := Left.Disk].composeContinuous (Cylinder.topology topology) topology Left.diskTopology Cylinder.point first (Cylinder.pointContinuous topology) (Product.Maps[].firstContinuous Left.diskTopology Right.diskTopology));
\definition rightCylinderContinuous: topology.Continuity[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := RC.Carrier].Continuous (Cylinder.topology topology) (RC.topology Right.diskTopology) rightCylinder :=
  topological_constructions.Product[A := J.Carrier, B := Right.Disk].Universal[X := Cylinder.Carrier].pairContinuous (Cylinder.topology topology) J.topology Right.diskTopology Cylinder.time (\fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier) => second (Cylinder.point p))
    (Cylinder.timeContinuous topology)
    (topology.Composition[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := Carrier, C := Right.Disk].composeContinuous (Cylinder.topology topology) topology Right.diskTopology Cylinder.point second (Cylinder.pointContinuous topology) (Product.Maps[].secondContinuous Left.diskTopology Right.diskTopology));
\definition motion[p: Cylinder.Carrier]: Carrier := Pair.pair Left.Disk Right.Disk (LM.motion (leftCylinder p)) (RM.motion (rightCylinder p));
\definition continuous: topology.Continuity[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := Carrier].Continuous (Cylinder.topology topology) topology motion :=
  Product.Universal[X := Cylinder.Carrier].pairContinuous (Cylinder.topology topology) Left.diskTopology Right.diskTopology (\fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier) => LM.motion (leftCylinder p)) (\fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier) => RM.motion (rightCylinder p))
    (topology.Composition[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := LC.Carrier, C := Left.Disk].composeContinuous (Cylinder.topology topology) (LC.topology Left.diskTopology) Left.diskTopology leftCylinder LM.motion leftCylinderContinuous LM.continuous)
    (topology.Composition[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := RC.Carrier, C := Right.Disk].composeContinuous (Cylinder.topology topology) (RC.topology Right.diskTopology) Right.diskTopology rightCylinder RM.motion rightCylinderContinuous RM.continuous);
\import algebraic_topology.Homotopy[A := Carrier, B := Carrier] \as Homotopies;
\definition deformation: Homotopies.Homotopy[topology, topology, identity, clip] := \into[Homotopies.Family](motion, Homotopies.Homotopy[topology, topology, identity, clip]) \by {
  Homotopies.Laws[topology, topology, identity, clip, motion] {
    continuous := continuous,
    start := \fun (p: Carrier) => Pair.Prop[A := Left.Disk, B := Right.Disk].ext _ _ (LM.start (first p)) (RM.start (second p)),
    end := \fun (p: Carrier) => \refl(clip p)
  }
};
\definition closedFirstPreserved: \forall (t: J.Carrier)(p: Carrier) -> \In[Carrier] closedFirst p -> \In[Carrier] closedFirst (motion (Cylinder.at t p)) :=
  \fun (t: J.Carrier)(p: Carrier)(inside: _) => \idelim first p = LM.motion (LC.at t (first p)) \with v: Left.Disk => \In[Left.Disk] L.boundary v \by {
    base: inside, equality: sym!{{Left.Disk}} _ _ (LM.fixed t (\into[Left.Disk](first p, L.boundary) \by { inside }))
  };
\definition closedSecondPreserved: \forall (t: J.Carrier)(p: Carrier) -> \In[Carrier] closedSecond p -> \In[Carrier] closedSecond (motion (Cylinder.at t p)) :=
  \fun (t: J.Carrier)(p: Carrier)(inside: _) => \idelim second p = RM.motion (RC.at t (second p)) \with v: Right.Disk => \In[Right.Disk] R.boundary v \by {
    base: inside, equality: sym!{{Right.Disk}} _ _ (RM.fixed t (\into[Right.Disk](second p, R.boundary) \by { inside }))
  };
\definition shellFirstPreserved: \forall (t: J.Carrier)(p: Carrier) -> \In[Carrier] shellFirst p -> \In[Carrier] shellFirst (motion (Cylinder.at t p)) :=
  \fun (t: J.Carrier)(p: Carrier)(inside: _) => LM.preservesShell t (\into[Left.Disk](first p, L.shell) \by { inside });
\definition shellSecondPreserved: \forall (t: J.Carrier)(p: Carrier) -> \In[Carrier] shellSecond p -> \In[Carrier] shellSecond (motion (Cylinder.at t p)) :=
  \fun (t: J.Carrier)(p: Carrier)(inside: _) => RM.preservesShell t (\into[Right.Disk](second p, R.shell) \by { inside });
\module Families;
\module Pair;

\import algebraic_topology.Interval[] \as J;
\import algebraic_topology.Cylinder[A := Euclidean.Disk] \as Cylinder;
\import algebraic_topology.Cylinder[A := Disk.DiskBundle] \as NativeCylinder;
\definition intoNative[p: Cylinder.Carrier]: NativeCylinder.Carrier := NativeCylinder.at (Cylinder.time p) (backward (Cylinder.point p));
\definition intoNativeContinuous: topology.Continuity[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := NativeCylinder.Carrier].Continuous (Cylinder.topology Euclidean.diskTopology) (NativeCylinder.topology Disk.diskTopology) intoNative :=
  topological_constructions.Product[A := J.Carrier, B := Disk.DiskBundle].Universal[X := Cylinder.Carrier].pairContinuous (Cylinder.topology Euclidean.diskTopology) J.topology Disk.diskTopology Cylinder.time (\fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier) => backward (Cylinder.point p))
    (Cylinder.timeContinuous Euclidean.diskTopology)
    (topology.Composition[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := Euclidean.Disk, C := Disk.DiskBundle].composeContinuous (Cylinder.topology Euclidean.diskTopology) Euclidean.diskTopology Disk.diskTopology Cylinder.point backward (Cylinder.pointContinuous Euclidean.diskTopology) backwardContinuous);
\definition motion[p: Cylinder.Carrier]: Euclidean.Disk := forward (Shell.homotopy (intoNative p));
\definition continuous: topology.Continuity[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := Euclidean.Disk].Continuous (Cylinder.topology Euclidean.diskTopology) Euclidean.diskTopology motion :=
  topology.Composition[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := Disk.DiskBundle, C := Euclidean.Disk].composeContinuous (Cylinder.topology Euclidean.diskTopology) Disk.diskTopology Euclidean.diskTopology (\fun (p: Cylinder.Carrier) => Shell.homotopy (intoNative p)) forward
    (topology.Composition[A := Cylinder.Carrier, B := NativeCylinder.Carrier, C := Disk.DiskBundle].composeContinuous (Cylinder.topology Euclidean.diskTopology) (NativeCylinder.topology Disk.diskTopology) Disk.diskTopology intoNative Shell.homotopy intoNativeContinuous Shell.continuous) forwardContinuous;
\definition start: \forall (v: Euclidean.Disk) -> motion (Cylinder.at J.start v) = v :=
  \fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => trans!{{Euclidean.Disk}} _ _ _ (congr!{{Disk.DiskBundle} {Euclidean.Disk}} forward _ _ (Shell.start (backward v))) (rightInverse v);
\definition clip[v: Euclidean.Disk]: Euclidean.Disk := motion (Cylinder.at J.end v);
\definition clipContinuous: topology.Continuity[A := Euclidean.Disk, B := Euclidean.Disk].Continuous Euclidean.diskTopology Euclidean.diskTopology clip :=
  topology.Composition[A := Euclidean.Disk, B := Cylinder.Carrier, C := Euclidean.Disk].composeContinuous Euclidean.diskTopology (Cylinder.topology Euclidean.diskTopology) Euclidean.diskTopology (Cylinder.at J.end) motion (Cylinder.atContinuous Euclidean.diskTopology J.end) continuous;
\definition fixed: \forall (t: J.Carrier)(v: \Cast[Euclidean.Disk] boundary) -> motion (Cylinder.at t v) = v :=
  \fun (t: J.Carrier)(v: \Cast[Euclidean.Disk] boundary) => trans!{{Euclidean.Disk}} _ _ _
    (congr!{{Disk.DiskBundle} {Euclidean.Disk}} forward _ _ (Shell.fixed t (\into[Disk.DiskBundle](backward v, Disk.Boundary) \by { boundaryPair.backwardPreserves v }))) (rightInverse v);
\definition preservesShell: \forall (t: J.Carrier)(v: \Cast[Euclidean.Disk] shell) -> \In[Euclidean.Disk] shell (motion (Cylinder.at t v)) :=
  \fun (t: J.Carrier)(v: \Cast[Euclidean.Disk] shell) => shellPair.forwardPreserves
    (\into[Disk.DiskBundle](Shell.homotopy (NativeCylinder.at t (backward v)), Shell.shell) \by {
      Shell.preservesShell t (\into[Disk.DiskBundle](backward v, Shell.shell) \by { shellPair.backwardPreserves v })
    });
\definition endsInBoundary: \forall (v: \Cast[Euclidean.Disk] shell) -> \In[Euclidean.Disk] boundary (clip v) :=
  \fun (v: \Cast[Euclidean.Disk] shell) => boundaryPair.forwardPreserves
    (\into[Disk.DiskBundle](Shell.clip (backward v), Disk.Boundary) \by {
      Shell.endsInBoundary (\into[Disk.DiskBundle](backward v, Shell.shell) \by { shellPair.backwardPreserves v })
    });
\import algebraic_topology.Homotopy[A := Euclidean.Disk, B := Euclidean.Disk] \as Homotopies;
\definition deformation: Homotopies.Homotopy[Euclidean.diskTopology, Euclidean.diskTopology, (\fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => v), clip] :=
  \into[Homotopies.Family](motion, Homotopies.Homotopy[Euclidean.diskTopology, Euclidean.diskTopology, (\fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => v), clip]) \by {
    Homotopies.Laws[Euclidean.diskTopology, Euclidean.diskTopology, (\fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => v), clip, motion] { continuous := continuous, start := start, end := \fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => \refl(clip v) }
  };
\definition reverse: Homotopies.Homotopy[Euclidean.diskTopology, Euclidean.diskTopology, clip, (\fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => v)] := Homotopies.reverse Euclidean.diskTopology Euclidean.diskTopology (\fun (v: Euclidean.Disk) => v) clip deformation;

* inclusionLaw, classLaw for arbitrary standard point cohomology class;
* canonical: factor class of standard generator equals ACTUAL chart-open canonical class;
* negative and negativeCanonical via actual induced module homomorphism.

Restriction.formula then combines this with existing Comparison.law and explicit equalities of factor restrictions to arbitrary standard point lifts; it concludes coordinate pull of StandardProduct.apply. It does not assume product primitivity, Thom theorem, or normalization theorem.

\import std.Arithmetic[].Int[] \as I;
\import std.Arithmetic[].IntAlgebra[].Ring[] \as Integers;
\import std.Logic[].Equality[] \as Equality;
\use Equality.trans;
\use Equality.sym;
\use Equality.congr;
\use Equality.eq_reason_in;
\import module_theory.Module[].Over[R := I.Int^, ring := Integers.ring] \as Modules;
\definition one: I.Int^ := Integers.ring.one;
\definition rightOne: \forall (r: I.Int^) -> Modules.scalarModule.smul r one = r :=
  \fun (r: I.Int^) => trans!{I.Int^} _ _ _ (Integers.commutativeRing.mulCommutative r one) (Modules.scalarModule.scalarOne r);
\definition sourceExpansion: \forall (r: I.Int^) -> eA.forward.apply r = A.space.smul r u :=
  \fun (r: I.Int^) => trans!{A.Carrier} _ _ _
    (sym!{A.Carrier} _ _ (congr!{I.Int^ A.Carrier} eA.forward.apply _ _ (rightOne r)))
    (trans!{A.Carrier} _ _ _ (eA.forward.scalar r one) (congr!{A.Carrier A.Carrier} (A.space.smul r) _ _ sourceUnit));
\definition targetExpansion: \forall (r: I.Int^) -> eB.forward.apply r = B.space.smul r (f.apply u) :=
  \fun (r: I.Int^) => trans!{B.Carrier} _ _ _
    (sym!{B.Carrier} _ _ (congr!{I.Int^ B.Carrier} eB.forward.apply _ _ (rightOne r)))
    (trans!{B.Carrier} _ _ _ (eB.forward.scalar r one) (congr!{B.Carrier B.Carrier} (B.space.smul r) _ _ targetUnit));
\definition onCoordinates: \forall (r: I.Int^) -> f.apply (eA.forward.apply r) = eB.forward.apply r :=
  \fun (r: I.Int^) => eq_reason_in!{B.Carrier _
    "=" _ "by" { congr!{A.Carrier B.Carrier} f.apply _ _ (sourceExpansion r) }
    "=" _ "by" { f.scalar r u }
    "=" _ "by" { sym!{B.Carrier} _ _ (targetExpansion r) }
  };
\import Modules.Isomorphism[V := I.Int^, W := A.Carrier, source := Modules.scalarModule, target := A.space].Inverse[] \as SourceInverse;
\import Modules.Isomorphism[V := A.Carrier, W := I.Int^, source := A.space, target := Modules.scalarModule].Composition[U := B.Carrier, final := B.space] \as Composition;
\definition conjugate: Modules.Isomorphism[V := A.Carrier, W := B.Carrier, source := A.space, target := B.space].Isomorphism :=
  Composition.compose eB (SourceInverse.inverse eA);
\definition formula: \forall (x: A.Carrier) -> f.apply x = conjugate.forward.apply x :=
  \fun (x: A.Carrier) => trans!{B.Carrier} _ _ _
    (sym!{B.Carrier} _ _ (congr!{A.Carrier B.Carrier} f.apply _ _ (eA.rightInverse x))) (onCoordinates (eA.backward.apply x));
\definition isomorphism: Modules.Isomorphism[V := A.Carrier, W := B.Carrier, source := A.space, target := B.space].Isomorphism :=
  Modules.Isomorphism[V := A.Carrier, W := B.Carrier, source := A.space, target := B.space].Isomorphism {
    forward := f, backward := conjugate.backward,
    leftInverse := \fun (x: A.Carrier) => trans!{A.Carrier} _ _ _ (congr!{B.Carrier A.Carrier} conjugate.backward.apply _ _ (formula x)) (conjugate.leftInverse x),
    rightInverse := \fun (x: B.Carrier) => trans!{B.Carrier} _ _ _ (formula (conjugate.backward.apply x)) (conjugate.rightInverse x)
  };

\import std.Data[].Nat[] \as N;
\import std.Arithmetic[].Int[] \as I;
\import std.Arithmetic[].IntAlgebra[].Ring[] \as Integers;
\import std.Logic[].Equality[] \as Equality;
\use Equality.trans;
\use Equality.sym;
\use Equality.congr;
\use Equality.eq_reason_in;
\import module_theory.Module[].Over[R := I.Int^, ring := Integers.ring] \as Modules;
\import Modules.Alternating[V := D.Carrier, space := D.space].Concatenation[] \as Signs;
\import Modules.Isomorphism[V := A.Carrier, W := B.Carrier, source := A.space, target := B.space].Inverse[] \as SourceInverse;
\import Modules.Isomorphism[V := B.Carrier, W := A.Carrier, source := B.space, target := A.space].Composition[U := C.Carrier, final := C.space] \as Before;
\import Modules.Isomorphism[V := B.Carrier, W := C.Carrier, source := B.space, target := C.space].Composition[U := D.Carrier, final := D.space] \as After;
\definition conjugate: Modules.Isomorphism[V := B.Carrier, W := D.Carrier, source := B.space, target := D.space].Isomorphism :=
  After.compose targetIso (Before.compose lowerIso (SourceInverse.inverse sourceIso));
\definition formula: \forall (x: B.Carrier) -> conjugate.forward.apply x = Signs.twist p (upper.apply x) :=
  \fun (x: B.Carrier) => trans!{D.Carrier} _ _ _ (comparison (sourceIso.backward.apply x))
    (congr!{B.Carrier D.Carrier} (\fun (v: B.Carrier) => Signs.twist p (upper.apply v)) _ _ (sourceIso.rightInverse x));
\definition inverse[y: D.Carrier]: B.Carrier := conjugate.backward.apply (Signs.twist p y);
\definition leftInverse: \forall (x: B.Carrier) -> inverse (upper.apply x) = x :=
  \fun (x: B.Carrier) => trans!{B.Carrier} _ _ _
    (congr!{D.Carrier B.Carrier} conjugate.backward.apply _ _ (sym!{D.Carrier} _ _ (formula x))) (conjugate.leftInverse x);
\definition rightInverse: \forall (y: D.Carrier) -> upper.apply (inverse y) = y :=
  \fun (y: D.Carrier) => eq_reason_in!{D.Carrier _
    "=" _ "by" { sym!{D.Carrier} _ _ (Signs.involution p (upper.apply (inverse y))) }
    "=" _ "by" { congr!{D.Carrier D.Carrier} (Signs.twist p) _ _ (sym!{D.Carrier} _ _ (formula (inverse y))) }
    "=" _ "by" { congr!{D.Carrier D.Carrier} (Signs.twist p) _ _ (conjugate.rightInverse (Signs.twist p y)) }
    "=" _ "by" { Signs.involution p y }
  };
\import Modules.Hom[V := D.Carrier, W := B.Carrier, source := D.space, target := B.space] \as BackwardMaps;
\definition backward: BackwardMaps.Map := BackwardMaps.Map {
  apply := inverse,
  addition := \fun (x, y: D.Carrier) => trans!{B.Carrier} _ _ _
    (congr!{D.Carrier B.Carrier} conjugate.backward.apply _ _ (Signs.twistAdd p x y)) (conjugate.backward.addition (Signs.twist p x) (Signs.twist p y)),
  scalar := \fun (r: I.Int^)(x: D.Carrier) => trans!{B.Carrier} _ _ _
    (congr!{D.Carrier B.Carrier} conjugate.backward.apply _ _ (Signs.twistScalar p r x)) (conjugate.backward.scalar r (Signs.twist p x))
};
\definition isomorphism: Modules.Isomorphism[V := B.Carrier, W := D.Carrier, source := B.space, target := D.space].Isomorphism :=
  Modules.Isomorphism[V := B.Carrier, W := D.Carrier, source := B.space, target := D.space].Isomorphism {
    forward := upper, backward := backward, leftInverse := leftInverse, rightInverse := rightInverse
  };

\definition firstPart: \Pow A := Sets.Preimage f first;
\definition secondPart: \Pow A := Sets.Preimage f second;
\definition family: \Pow (\Pow A) := Sets.PullbackFamily f original;
\definition firstMember: \In[\Pow A] family firstPart := Sets.pullbackMember f original first (Logic.Or[_, _]::left (\refl(first)));

\definition classify: \forall (U: \Pow A) -> \In[\Pow A] family U -> Logic.Or[U = firstPart, U = secondPart] :=
  \fun (U: \Pow A)(member: \In[\Pow A] family U) => Sets.pullbackElim f original U (Logic.Or[U = firstPart, U = secondPart])

      {\fun (left: V = first) => Logic.Or[_, _]::left (trans!{{\Pow A}} _ _ _ same (congr!{{\Pow B} {\Pow A}} (Sets.Preimage f) _ _ left))}
      {\fun (right: V = second) => Logic.Or[_, _]::right (trans!{{\Pow A}} _ _ _ same (congr!{{\Pow B} {\Pow A}} (Sets.Preimage f) _ _ right))}}) member;

\import \parent.SumComparison[] \as Comparison;
\import \parent.Naturality[] \as Naturality;
\definition sourceA: Modules.Bundle := Modules.Bundle { Carrier := OF.Carrier, space := OF.space };
\definition sourceB: Modules.Bundle := Modules.Bundle { Carrier := FreeSum.Carrier, space := FreeSum.space };
\definition sourceC: Modules.Bundle := Modules.Bundle { Carrier := WF.Carrier, space := WF.space };
\definition targetA: Modules.Bundle := Modules.Bundle { Carrier := O.Carrier index, space := O.space index };
\definition targetB: Modules.Bundle := Modules.Bundle { Carrier := Sum.Carrier index, space := Sum.space index };
\definition targetC: Modules.Bundle := Modules.Bundle { Carrier := SmallPair.complex.family.Carrier index, space := SmallPair.complex.family.space index };
\import Modules.ExactPair[A := sourceA, B := sourceB, C := sourceC] \as Original;
\definition original: Original.Sequence := Original.Sequence {
  first := Free.inclusion, last := Free.projection, zero := Free.sequence.zero, kernelImage := Free.sequence.kernelImage
};
\import Chains.Map[C := O.complex, D := Sum.complex].At[k := index] \as Inclusion;
\import Chains.Map[C := Sum.complex, D := SmallPair.complex].At[k := index] \as Projection;
\import Original.Transport[A1 := targetA, B1 := targetB, C1 := targetC, sequence := original,
  f := Inclusion.linear inclusion, g := Projection.linear projection,
  a := overlapIsomorphism, b := Comparison.isomorphism, c := wholeIsomorphism] \as Transport;
\definition firstCommutes: Transport.FirstCommutes := \fun (x: OF.Carrier) => sym!{{Sum.Carrier index}} _ _ (Naturality.inclusionSquare x);
\definition lastCommutes: Transport.LastCommutes := \fun (x: FreeSum.Carrier) => sym!{{SmallPair.complex.family.Carrier index}} _ _ (Naturality.projectionSquare x);
\definition zero: _ := Transport.zero firstCommutes lastCommutes;
\definition kernelImage: _ := Transport.kernelImage firstCommutes lastCommutes;
\definition backwardSquare: \forall (x: O.Carrier index) -> Comparison.backward.apply (inclusion.apply index x) = Free.inclusion.apply (overlapIsomorphism.backward.apply x) :=
  \fun (x: O.Carrier index) => trans!{FreeSum.Carrier} _ _ _
    (congr!{{Sum.Carrier index} FreeSum.Carrier} Comparison.backward.apply _ _ (Transport.firstInverse firstCommutes x))
    (Comparison.isomorphism.leftInverse (Free.inclusion.apply (overlapIsomorphism.backward.apply x)));
\definition injective: \forall (x, y: O.Carrier index) -> inclusion.apply index x = inclusion.apply index y -> x = y :=
  \fun (x, y: O.Carrier index)(same: _) => eq_reason_in!{{O.Carrier index} _
    "=" _ "by" { sym!{{O.Carrier index}} _ _ (overlapIsomorphism.rightInverse x) }
    "=" _ "by" { congr!{OF.Carrier {O.Carrier index}} overlapIsomorphism.forward.apply _ _
      (Free.sequence.injective _ _ (eq_reason_in!{FreeSum.Carrier _
        "=" _ "by" { sym!{FreeSum.Carrier} _ _ (backwardSquare x) }
        "=" _ "by" { congr!{{Sum.Carrier index} FreeSum.Carrier} Comparison.backward.apply _ _ same }
        "=" _ "by" { backwardSquare y }
      })) }
    "=" _ "by" { overlapIsomorphism.rightInverse y }
  };
\definition onto: \forall (z: targetC.Carrier) -> \exists ( y: targetB.Carrier \where projection.apply index y = z ) := \block {
  \fun (z: targetC.Carrier) \then
  \takefrom x: \Cast[FreeSum.Carrier] ({ x: FreeSum.Carrier \where Free.projection.apply x = wholeIsomorphism.backward.apply z })
    \by Free.sequence.onto (wholeIsomorphism.backward.apply z) \then
  \return Logic.existsIntro[targetB.Carrier, { y: targetB.Carrier \where projection.apply index y = z }] (Comparison.forward.apply x)
    (eq_reason_in!{targetC.Carrier _
      "=" _ "by" { Naturality.projectionSquare x }
      "=" _ "by" { congr!{WF.Carrier targetC.Carrier} wholeIsomorphism.forward.apply _ _
        (\bysub(FreeSum.Carrier, { x: FreeSum.Carrier \where Free.projection.apply x = wholeIsomorphism.backward.apply z }, x)) }
      "=" _ "by" { wholeIsomorphism.rightInverse z }
    })
};

\import Modules.Hom[V := OF.Carrier, W := targetA.Carrier, source := OF.space, target := targetA.space]
  .Composition[U := targetB.Carrier, domain := targetB.space] \as Last;
\definition retraction: Modules.Hom[V := targetB.Carrier, W := targetA.Carrier, source := targetB.space, target := targetA.space].Map :=
  Last.compose overlapIsomorphism.forward (First.compose Free.Retraction[].map Comparison.backward);
\definition leftInverse: \forall (x: targetA.Carrier) -> retraction.apply (inclusion.apply index x) = x :=
  \fun (x: targetA.Carrier) => eq_reason_in!{targetA.Carrier _
    "=" _ "by" { congr!{FreeSum.Carrier targetA.Carrier} (\fun (y: FreeSum.Carrier) => overlapIsomorphism.forward.apply (Free.Retraction[].map.apply y)) _ _ (backwardSquare x) }

    "=" _ "by" { overlapIsomorphism.rightInverse x }
  };

\import topology.Topology[Carrier := UnionRelative.SubspaceCarrier] \as UnionTop;
\import topology.Topology[Carrier := A].Subspace[S := Union.subset] \as UnionSpace;
\definition firstOfUnion: \forall (x: A) -> \In[A] Union.subset x -> \In[A] first x :=
  \fun (x: A)(inside: _) => either!{inside {\In[A] first x}
    {\fun (left: \In[A] first x) => left}
    {\fun (right: \In[A] second x) => Logic.falseElim _ (empty x right)}};
\definition leftWhole: Cover.left = UnionTop.Whole := \axiom:setext(Cover.left, UnionTop.Whole,
  (\fun (x: UnionRelative.SubspaceCarrier)(inside: \In[A] first x) => \refl(x)),
  (\fun (x: UnionRelative.SubspaceCarrier)(inside: x = x) => firstOfUnion x (\bysub(A, Union.subset, x))));
\definition rightEmpty: Cover.right = UnionTop.Empty := \axiom:setext(Cover.right, UnionTop.Empty,
  (\fun (x: UnionRelative.SubspaceCarrier)(inside: \In[A] second x) => empty x inside),
  (\fun (x: UnionRelative.SubspaceCarrier)(inside: Logic.False) => Logic.falseElim _ inside));
\definition conditions: Cover.Conditions := Cover.Conditions {
  leftOpen := \idelim UnionTop.Whole = Cover.left \with U: \Pow UnionRelative.SubspaceCarrier => UnionTop.IsOpenIn (UnionSpace.topology source) U \by {
    base: (UnionTop.topologyLaws (UnionSpace.topology source)).wholeOpen, equality: sym!{{\Pow UnionRelative.SubspaceCarrier}} _ _ leftWhole
  },
  rightOpen := \idelim UnionTop.Empty = Cover.right \with U: \Pow UnionRelative.SubspaceCarrier => UnionTop.IsOpenIn (UnionSpace.topology source) U \by {
    base: (UnionTop.topologyLaws (UnionSpace.topology source)).emptyOpen, equality: sym!{{\Pow UnionRelative.SubspaceCarrier}} _ _ rightEmpty
  }
};

  homeomorphism := topology.Homeomorphism[A := A, B := A].Homeomorphism[source, source] {
    forward := (\fun (x: A) => x), backward := (\fun (x: A) => x),
    leftInverse := (\fun (x: A) => \refl(x)), rightInverse := (\fun (x: A) => \refl(x)),
    forwardContinuous := topology.Maps[A := A, B := A].Identity[].continuous source,
    backwardContinuous := topology.Maps[A := A, B := A].Identity[].continuous source
  },
  forwardPreserves := (\fun (x: \Cast[A] Union.subset) => firstOfUnion x (\bysub(A, Union.subset, x))),
  backwardPreserves := (\fun (x: \Cast[A] first) => Logic.Or[_, _]::left (\bysub(A, first, x)))

  \import Integer.At[C := BaseCochains.complex, z := z] \as Input;
  \import Integer.At[C := OverlapCochains.complex, z := z] \as Output;
  \definition forward: _ := ProjectionMap.With[coefficients := coefficients].At[z := z].induced;
  \definition backward: _ := SectionMap.With[coefficients := coefficients].At[z := z].induced;
  \definition leftInverse: \forall (q: Input.Cohomology) -> backward.apply (forward.apply q) = q :=
    \fun (q: Input.Cohomology) => trans!{Input.Cohomology} _ _ _
      (sym!{Input.Cohomology} _ _ (OnBase.With[coefficients := coefficients].law z q))
      (BaseIdentity.With[coefficients := coefficients].law z q);
  \definition rightInverse: \forall (q: Output.Cohomology) -> forward.apply (backward.apply q) = q :=
    \fun (q: Output.Cohomology) => trans!{Output.Cohomology} _ _ _
      (sym!{Output.Cohomology} _ _ (OnOverlap.With[coefficients := coefficients].law z q))
      (trans!{Output.Cohomology} _ _ _ (Comparison.With[coefficients := coefficients].At[z := z].law q)
        (OverlapIdentity.With[coefficients := coefficients].law z q));
  \definition isomorphism: Modules.Isomorphism[V := Input.Cohomology, W := Output.Cohomology, source := Input.cohomologySpace, target := Output.cohomologySpace].Isomorphism :=
    Modules.Isomorphism[V := Input.Cohomology, W := Output.Cohomology, source := Input.cohomologySpace, target := Output.cohomologySpace].Isomorphism {
      forward := forward, backward := backward, leftInverse := leftInverse, rightInverse := rightInverse
    };
}

  }
};

      "=" _ "by" { DifferentialSigns.law Differential.differential p (Lower.apply a lift) }

  represents: Input.classOf x = u,
  value: Output.classOf (Upper.Cycles[].cycle a x) = v,

\definition uniqueness: \forall (a: Restriction.Cycle)(u: Input.Cohomology)(v, w: Value a u) -> v = w := \block {
  \fun (a: Restriction.Cycle)(u: Input.Cohomology)(v, w: Value a u) \then

  \let xl: Witness[a, u, v, x] := \bysub(Input.Closed, { x: Input.Closed \where Witness[a, u, v, x] }, x) \then

    base: \bysub(WholeSimplices.Carrier n, LeftSimplices.Small n, tau), equality: \bysub(LeftSimplices.Carrier n, LeftProjection.Fiber sigma, tau)

      "=" _ "by" { congr!{{Carrier n} {Whole.Carrier n}} linear.apply _ _ (Projection.outside sigma outside) }

\definition selected[n: N.Nat^]: \Pow (RightSimplices.Carrier n) := { sigma: RightSimplices.Carrier n \where \In[WholeSimplices.Carrier n] (LeftSimplices.Small n) sigma };
\definition Carrier[n: N.Nat^]: \Set := \Cast[RightSimplices.Carrier n] (selected n);
\definition face[n: N.Nat^][i: Fin.Fin (N.Nat^::succ (N.Nat^::succ n))][sigma: Carrier (N.Nat^::succ n)]: Carrier n :=
  \into[RightSimplices.Carrier n](RightSimplices.face n i sigma, selected n) \by {
    LeftSimplices.faceSmall n i (\into[WholeSimplices.Carrier (N.Nat^::succ n)](sigma, LeftSimplices.Small (N.Nat^::succ n)) \by {
      \bysub(RightSimplices.Carrier (N.Nat^::succ n), selected (N.Nat^::succ n), sigma)
    })
  };
\definition family: Semisimplicial.Family := Semisimplicial.Family { Carrier := Carrier };
\definition faces: Semisimplicial.Faces[family] := Semisimplicial.Faces[family] { apply := face };
\definition object: Semisimplicial.Object := Semisimplicial.Object {
  family := family, faces := faces,
  laws := Semisimplicial.Laws[family, faces] {
    identity := \fun (n: N.Nat^)(i, j: Fin.Fin (N.Nat^::succ (N.Nat^::succ n)))(sigma: Carrier (N.Nat^::succ (N.Nat^::succ n)))(ordered: N.Basic[].Prop[].Le i j) => WholeSimplices.Identities[].faceIdentity n i j sigma ordered
  }
};

\import Modules.Free[Generators := BoundarySimplices.Carrier n].Induction[].Into[W := Whole.Carrier (N.Nat^::succ n), target := Whole.space (N.Nat^::succ n)] \as Induction;
\definition onGenerator: \forall (sigma: BoundarySimplices.Carrier n) -> Higher.Image (map.apply (Boundary.generator n sigma)) :=
  \fun (sigma: BoundarySimplices.Carrier n) => transport!{{Whole.Carrier (N.Nat^::succ n)}} Higher.Image _ _
    (sym!{{Whole.Carrier (N.Nat^::succ n)}} _ _ (congr!{{Whole.Carrier n} {Whole.Carrier (N.Nat^::succ n)}} f.apply _ _ (Lower.onGenerator sigma))) (generators sigma);
\definition onBoundary: \forall (x: Boundary.Carrier n) -> Higher.Image (map.apply x) := Induction.law map Higher.submodule onGenerator;
\definition law: Preserves f := \block {
  \fun (x: Whole.Carrier n)(member: Lower.Image x) \then
  \takefrom y: \Cast[Boundary.Carrier n] ({ y: Boundary.Carrier n \where Lower.inclusion.apply y = x }) \by member \then
  \let same: Lower.inclusion.apply y = x := \bysub(Boundary.Carrier n, { y: Boundary.Carrier n \where Lower.inclusion.apply y = x }, y) \then
  \return transport!{{Whole.Carrier (N.Nat^::succ n)}} Higher.Image _ _ (congr!{{Whole.Carrier n} {Whole.Carrier (N.Nat^::succ n)}} f.apply _ _ same) (onBoundary y)
};

\definition residual: \forall (sigma: BoundarySimplices.Carrier (N.Nat^::succ n)) -> Current.Image (Construction.residual (Cover.homotopyMap n) sigma) :=
  \fun (sigma: BoundarySimplices.Carrier (N.Nat^::succ n)) => Current.submodule.addition _ _
    (Current.generatorImage sigma)
    (previous ((Whole.boundary n).apply (Whole.generator (N.Nat^::succ n) sigma))
      (\parent.BoundaryMap[n := n].law (Whole.generator (N.Nat^::succ n) sigma) (Current.generatorImage sigma)));
\definition onGenerator: \forall (sigma: BoundarySimplices.Carrier (N.Nat^::succ n)) -> Higher.Image ((Cover.homotopyMap (N.Nat^::succ n)).apply (Whole.generator (N.Nat^::succ n) sigma)) :=

      (Cover.Selection[n := N.Nat^::succ n].minimum (Construction.residual (Cover.homotopyMap n) sigma))
      (Construction.residual (Cover.homotopyMap n) sigma) (residual sigma));
\definition law: Property (N.Nat^::succ n) := \parent.Degree[n := N.Nat^::succ n].On[f := Cover.homotopyMap (N.Nat^::succ n), generators := onGenerator].law;
